## 13. Department Arena Cards

### Analytics Arena — THE ANALYTICS FORGE
- **Type:** Psychic / Steel
- **Gym Leader:** Leo Liu, Senior Manager Product Analytics
- **Challenge Rating:** MEDIUM -- Priorities 1-2 well-covered, but Reliability (P5) under-invested and DIM_TEAMS_DAILY_V2 spine bug blocks downstream
- **Terrain Effect:** Copilot Amplification -- all AI-assisted queries deal 1.5x damage. MCP connector grants a bonus turn at the start of battle. Claude Code users get permanent haste.
- **AOP Badge:** The NRR Intelligence Badge -- earned by building the NRR Intelligence Engine that ties churn, expansion, and product usage into a single story
- **Active Challengers:** Everyone. This is home turf. Leo (Dragon/Psychic), Bridie (Steel/Electric), Kirk (Psychic/Fighting), all AEs and DSes. Jarvis has permanent home-field advantage.
- **Known Hazard:** Bus Factor Trap -- SPOF trainers (Sai on AI, Mounica on Waterfall, Bridie on infrastructure) take 2x damage from AFK status. Henry's Legacy gives a 10% chance any cited metric is wrong. Decision Queue makes requests pile up faster than they're processed.
- **Flavor text:** The forge where every analytics asset is hammered into shape. 200 WAU target, 95%+ SLA, and the copilot program that's trying to make the whole company data-literate. The team that measures everyone else -- but struggles to measure itself.

---

### Growth & Acquisition Arena — THE CONVERSION FUNNEL
- **Type:** Fire / Grass
- **Gym Leader:** Dan Cronyn, VP Growth & Acquisition
- **Challenge Rating:** HIGH -- FTP cohorts, PQL/PQA, and F0-14 activation all missing from Snowflake. Committed AOP deliverables not being built.
- **Terrain Effect:** Funnel Acceleration -- activation-related moves gain +25% SP.ATK. But every metric must be validated against 3 different definitions before it counts.
- **AOP Badge:** The W2 FTP Badge -- earned by improving Free-to-Paid conversion from 4.0% to 4.5% with standardized cohort definitions
- **Active Challengers:** Anvitha (inbound), Adhiraj (activation -- partial), Tara (credit/pricing). Nobody covers Lifecycle or Pricing & Packaging full-time.
- **Known Hazard:** W2 FTP Definition Fog, Golden Population Shift, PLSM Handoff Gap.

---

### GTME Arena — THE EXPANSION CHAMBER
- **Type:** Fighting / Ground
- **Gym Leader:** Eric Quanstrom, VP GTM Engineering
- **Challenge Rating:** HIGH -- Q2 dashboard deadline approaching with zero visible build progress.
- **Terrain Effect:** Coverage Mandate -- every Custom/Org customer gets a GTME rep in FY27. Moves targeting uncovered accounts deal 3x damage.
- **AOP Badge:** The GRR Improvement Badge -- earned by proving the GTME model moves GRR from 61.2% to >70%
- **Active Challengers:** Kaitlyn (signal interventions), Shyam (churn risk scoring).
- **Known Hazard:** Credit Tracking Unreliability, no health scoring model, no intervention-to-ARR attribution.

---

### R&D / Product Arena — THE FEATURE FACTORY
- **Type:** Electric / Psychic
- **Gym Leader:** Bela Stepanova, Chief Product Officer
- **Challenge Rating:** HIGH -- COKR1 Win the Account ($100M) and COKR2 Deals/CI ($20M) have zero analytics support.
- **Terrain Effect:** Ship-It Momentum -- all trainers gain +15% SPD. But experiments must reach statistical significance or the move fizzles.
- **AOP Badge:** The Multi-Product Adoption Badge -- earned by increasing use cases per Paid WAT from 1.18 to 1.33
- **Active Challengers:** Sai (AI data layer -- COKR4), Kirk (DS experimentation), Adhiraj/Anvitha (COKR3 onboarding).
- **Known Hazard:** Amplitude Assignment Cache, Feature Flag Fog, AI Credit Type Mystery.

---

### Customer Care / Support Arena — THE SUPPORT TRENCHES
- **Type:** Water / Fairy
- **Gym Leader:** Kenny Keesee, VP Support
- **Challenge Rating:** MEDIUM -- solid analyst coverage but key metrics trapped in Intercom
- **Terrain Effect:** Customer Empathy Field -- all support-related moves gain +20% effectiveness.
- **AOP Badge:** The Save Motion Badge -- earned by building attribution from save motions to NRR impact
- **Active Challengers:** Marie (support analytics), Tighe (support pipeline + A360 dashboards).
- **Known Hazard:** Intercom Topic Nulls, Fivetran Sync Lag, CSAT Survivorship Bias.

---

### Partnerships Arena — THE ALLIANCE HALL
- **Type:** Ghost / Fairy
- **Gym Leader:** Jennifer Rhima, Director of Partnerships
- **Challenge Rating:** CRITICAL -- $7.2M FY27 target with zero dedicated analytics.
- **Terrain Effect:** Partner Multiplier -- all revenue attributed through partner channels deals 2x SP.ATK. Attribution is complex and the data pipeline barely exists.
- **AOP Badge:** The Partner Attribution Badge -- earned by building even basic tracking of partner-sourced vs partner-influenced ARR
- **Active Challengers:** Martin (de facto partnerships analyst -- 13 PartnerStack queries, the ONLY analytics touch).
- **Known Hazard:** Zero dedicated analytics support, no partner attribution pipeline, no dashboard.
